Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The relativity variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 69 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 396 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_69(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 69. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^396(S, F) is trivial. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
